====================================================================================================
QUESTION: If age was misstated in the application, how is the face amount adjusted?
CATEGORY: CAT3_CONDITIONAL_REASONING
EXPECTED ANSWER: The face amount is adjusted to what the premium would have purchased at the correct age.
EVIDENCE: 11.2 MISSTATEMENT OF AGE: If the insured's age was misstated, we will adjust the face amount to what the premium would have purchased at the correct age.
====================================================================================================
